    parent.scrollTop = itemTopRelative - parentRect.height / 2 + itemRect.height / 2;

class="mt-2 text-lg prose prose-gray dark:prose-invert [&amp;&gt;*]:[overflow-wrap:anywhere]"><p>ClickPesa makes accepting and disbursing payments easy, whether you know how to code or not - ClickPesa data-page-title="Quickstart" data-page-href="/home/introduction" id="content"><span data-as="p">Leverage our hosted or embedded functionality to go to market with your payments business quickly and securely. Power every payment flow with a single API. From pay-ins to pay-outs and everything in between, manage payment operations with ease.</span>
<h2 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="first-api-call"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#first-api-call" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">First API call</span></h2>
<span data-as="p">ClickPesa does not provide a sandbox. Every request, including the <a class="link" href="/api-reference">API Explorer</a>, runs against live production and moves real funds. Until <a class="link" href="/home/onboarding#limits-before-kyc-approval">KYC is approved</a> you are limited to <strong>TZS 100,000</strong> across collections, payouts, deposits, and withdraws, and <strong>100 API calls per day</strong> (including token generation).</span>
<span data-as="p">Create an application and copy your Client ID and API Key from <a class="link" href="/application/api-application-setup">API Integration Setup</a>. Generate a token once, reuse it for up to <strong>1 hour</strong>, and preview with a small amount before you initiate. Full testing guidance: <a class="link" href="/home/sandbox-and-testing-environment">Sandbox and Testing Environment</a>.</span>

<span class="line"><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#9CDCFE">$baseUrl</span><span style="color:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4"> =</span><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178"> &quot;https://api.clickpesa.com/third-parties&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">;</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#9CDCFE">$ch</span><span style="color:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4"> =</span><span style="color:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#DCDCAA"> curl_init</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">(</span><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#9CDCFE">$baseUrl</span><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">/generate-token&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#DCDCAA">curl_setopt_array</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">(</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#9CDCFE">$ch</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">, [</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">  CURLOPT_RETURNTRANSFER</span><span style="color:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4"> =&gt;</span><span style="color:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#569CD6"> true</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">  CURLOPT_POST</span><span style="color:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4"> =&gt;</span><span style="color:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#569CD6"> true</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">  CURLOPT_HTTPHEADER</span><span style="color:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4"> =&gt;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4"> [</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">    &quot;client-id: YOUR_CLIENT_ID&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">    &quot;api-key: YOUR_API_KEY&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">  ],</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">]);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#9CDCFE">$tokenResponse</span><span style="color:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4"> =</span><span style="color:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#DCDCAA"> json_decode</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">(</span><span style="color:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#DCDCAA">curl_exec</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">(</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#9CDCFE">$ch</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">), </span><span style="color:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#569CD6">true</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#DCDCAA">curl_close</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">(</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#9CDCFE">$ch</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">);</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#9CDCFE">$token</span><span style="color:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4"> =</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#9CDCFE"> $tokenResponse</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">[</span><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">&quot;token&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">]; </span><span style="color:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#6A9955">// already includes &quot;Bearer &quot;</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#9CDCFE">$payload</span><span style="color:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4"> =</span><span style="color:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#DCDCAA"> json_encode</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">([</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">  &quot;amount&quot;</span><span style="color:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4"> =&gt;</span><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178"> &quot;1000&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">  &quot;currency&quot;</span><span style="color:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4"> =&gt;</span><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178"> &quot;TZS&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">  &quot;orderReference&quot;</span><span style="color:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4"> =&gt;</span><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178"> &quot;TEST001&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">, </span><span style="color:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#6A9955">// alphanumeric, max 20 characters</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">  &quot;phoneNumber&quot;</span><span style="color:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4"> =&gt;</span><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178"> &quot;255712345678&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">]);</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#9CDCFE">$ch</span><span style="color:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4"> =</span><span style="color:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#DCDCAA"> curl_init</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">(</span><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#9CDCFE">$baseUrl</span><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">/payments/preview-ussd-push-request&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#DCDCAA">curl_setopt_array</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">(</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#9CDCFE">$ch</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">, [</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">  CURLOPT_RETURNTRANSFER</span><span style="color:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4"> =&gt;</span><span style="color:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#569CD6"> true</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">  CURLOPT_POST</span><span style="color:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4"> =&gt;</span><span style="color:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#569CD6"> true</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">  CURLOPT_HTTPHEADER</span><span style="color:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4"> =&gt;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4"> [</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">    &quot;Authorization: </span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#9CDCFE">$token</span><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">    &quot;Content-Type: application/json&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">  ],</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">  CURLOPT_POSTFIELDS</span><span style="color:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4"> =&gt;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#9CDCFE"> $payload</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">]);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#9CDCFE">$preview</span><span style="color:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4"> =</span><span style="color:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#DCDCAA"> curl_exec</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">(</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#9CDCFE">$ch</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#DCDCAA">curl_close</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">(</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#9CDCFE">$ch</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">);</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#DCDCAA">echo</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#9CDCFE"> $preview</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">;</span></span>
</code></pre></div></div></div></div><div class="code-block-fade-overlay print:hidden" data-fade-overlay="true" aria-hidden="true" style="--fade-color-light:#ffffff;--fade-color-dark:#0B0C0E"></div></div></div></div>
<span data-as="p"><code>orderReference</code> on USSD Push must be alphanumeric and at most <strong>20 characters</strong>. Prefer <a class="link" href="/api-reference/collection/ussd-push-requests/preview-ussd-push-request">preview</a> over <a class="link" href="/api-reference/collection/ussd-push-requests/initiate-ussd-push-request">initiate</a> until the request looks correct.</span>

dark:hover:border-primary-light!" aria-labelledby="_R_9kllktbsnlhjiuqsnpfiutb_" tabindex="0" role="link"><div class="px-6 py-5 relative" data-component-part="card-content-container"><div class="absolute text-gray-400 dark:text-gray-500 group-hover:text-primary dark:group-hover:text-primary-light top-5 right-5 hidden"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="m-0.5 size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M14.25 3.75L3.75525 14.2446" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M4.71301 3.75H14.2499V13.2869" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div><div aria-hidden="true" class="size-6 fill-gray-800 dark:fill-gray-100 text-gray-800 dark:text-gray-100 [&amp;&gt;svg]:size-6" data-component-part="card-icon"><svg class="forced-colors:forced-color-adjust-none forced-colors:bg-[color:CanvasText]! Embed it on your site or redirect customers to Hosted Checkout</span></div></div></div></div><div class="card block font-normal group relative my-2 ring-2 ring-transparent rounded-2xl bg-white dark:bg-background-dark border border-gray-950/10 dark:border-white/10 overflow-hidden w-full cursor-pointer hover:border-primary!
